Let&#x27;s go over each setting:</p><h4 id="less-than-enchantment_name-greater-than" class="text-xl font-semibold heading flex items-baseline scroll-mt-(--content-scroll-margin) text-start justify-self-start relative group/hash mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 column-first-of-type:pt-0 pt-[0.5em]"><div class="relative hash grid grid-area-1-1 h-[1em] border-0 opacity-0 group-hover/hash:opacity-[0] group-focus/hash:opacity-[0] md:group-hover/hash:opacity-[1] md:group-focus/hash:opacity-[1] -ml-6 pr-2"><a href="#less-than-enchantment_name-greater-than" aria-label="Direct link to heading" class="inline-flex h-full items-start leading-snug"><svg style="mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);-webkit-mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;-webkit-mask-position:center" class="gb-icon self-center transition-colors text-transparent group-hover/hash:text-tint-subtle contrast-more:group-hover/hash:text-tint-strong size-4"></svg></a></div><div class="flex-1 z-1 max-w-full break-words text-start justify-self-start leading-snug"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">&lt;enchantment_name&gt;</code></div></h4><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">This is the &quot;backend&quot; enchantment name, used in all commands and admin processes. The enchantment name must be written in lowercase.</p><h4 id="display" class="text-xl font-semibold heading flex items-baseline scroll-mt-(--content-scroll-margin) text-start justify-self-start relative group/hash mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 column-first-of-type:pt-0 pt-[0.5em]"><div class="relative hash grid grid-area-1-1 h-[1em] border-0 opacity-0 group-hover/hash:opacity-[0] group-focus/hash:opacity-[0] md:group-hover/hash:opacity-[1] md:group-focus/hash:opacity-[1] -ml-6 pr-2"><a href="#display" aria-label="Direct link to heading" class="inline-flex h-full items-start leading-snug"><svg style="mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);-webkit-mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;-webkit-mask-position:center" class="gb-icon self-center transition-colors text-transparent group-hover/hash:text-tint-subtle contrast-more:group-hover/hash:text-tint-strong size-4"></svg></a></div><div class="flex-1 z-1 max-w-full break-words text-start justify-self-start leading-snug"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">display</code></div></h4><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">This is the enchantment display, which appears in item lore, chat messages, and book formats. Color codes can be used with the display. Special placeholders include:</p><ul class="min-w-0 space-y-2 mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0"><li class="leading-normal class="page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl min-h-lh flip-heading-hash [h2]:pt-0 [h3]:pt-0 [h4]:pt-0 mx-0 text-start justify-self-start"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">%group-color%</code>: Use the group&#x27;s global color code.</p></div></li></ul><h4 id="description" class="text-xl font-semibold heading flex items-baseline scroll-mt-(--content-scroll-margin) text-start justify-self-start relative grid-area-1-1 h-[1em] border-0 opacity-0 group-hover/hash:opacity-[0] group-focus/hash:opacity-[0] md:group-hover/hash:opacity-[1] md:group-focus/hash:opacity-[1] -ml-6 pr-2"><a href="#description" leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">description</code></div></h4><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">This is the enchantment&#x27;s description, used in various menus and book formats. It supports the &quot;\n&quot; character for splitting to a new line, and requires double-quotes (&quot;) instead of single (&#x27;). Color codes can also be used with the display.</p><h4 id="applies-to" class="text-xl font-semibold heading flex items-baseline scroll-mt-(--content-scroll-margin) text-start justify-self-start relative group/hash mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 column-first-of-type:pt-0 pt-[0.5em]"><div class="relative hash grid grid-area-1-1 h-[1em] border-0 opacity-0 group-hover/hash:opacity-[0] group-focus/hash:opacity-[0] md:group-hover/hash:opacity-[1] md:group-focus/hash:opacity-[1] -ml-6 pr-2"><a href="#applies-to" aria-label="Direct link to heading" class="inline-flex h-full items-start leading-snug"><svg style="mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);-webkit-mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;-webkit-mask-position:center" class="gb-icon self-center transition-colors text-transparent group-hover/hash:text-tint-subtle contrast-more:group-hover/hash:text-tint-strong size-4"></svg></a></div><div class="flex-1 z-1 max-w-full break-words text-start justify-self-start leading-snug"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">applies-to</code></div></h4><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">This is used in menus, book formats, and chat messages. It tells users which item category the enchantment can be applied to (e.g., &#x27;Armour&#x27;, &#x27;Weapons&#x27;, etc.). Note that <code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">applies-to</code> is visual only and does not affect which items the enchantment can actually be added to.</p><h4 id="type" class="text-xl font-semibold heading flex items-baseline scroll-mt-(--content-scroll-margin) text-start justify-self-start relative group/hash mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 column-first-of-type:pt-0 pt-[0.5em]"><div class="relative hash grid grid-area-1-1 h-[1em] border-0 opacity-0 group-hover/hash:opacity-[0] group-focus/hash:opacity-[0] md:group-hover/hash:opacity-[1] md:group-focus/hash:opacity-[1] -ml-6 pr-2"><a href="#type" aria-label="Direct link to heading" class="inline-flex h-full items-start leading-snug"><svg style="mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);-webkit-mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;-webkit-mask-position:center" class="gb-icon self-center transition-colors text-transparent group-hover/hash:text-tint-subtle contrast-more:group-hover/hash:text-tint-strong size-4"></svg></a></div><div class="flex-1 z-1 max-w-full break-words text-start justify-self-start leading-snug"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">type</code></div></h4><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">This defines what triggers the enchantment. Enchantment triggers can be found <a class="underline decoration-[max(0.07em,1px)] underline-offset-2 links-accent:underline-offset-4 links-default:decoration-primary/6 links-default:text-primary-subtle hover:links-default:text-primary-strong contrast-more:links-default:text-primary contrast-more:hover:links-default:text-primary-strong links-accent:decoration-primary-subtle hover:links-accent:decoration-[3px] hover:links-accent:[text-decoration-skip-ink:none] transition-all duration-100" href="https://wiki.advancedplugins.net/abilities/triggers">here<svg style="mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/arrow-up-right.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);-webkit-mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/arrow-up-right.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;-webkit-mask-position:center" class="gb-icon ml-0.5 inline size-3 links-accent:text-tint-subtle"></svg></a></p><h4 id="group" class="text-xl font-semibold heading flex items-baseline scroll-mt-(--content-scroll-margin) text-start justify-self-start relative group/hash mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 column-first-of-type:pt-0 pt-[0.5em]"><div class="relative hash grid grid-area-1-1 h-[1em] border-0 opacity-0 group-hover/hash:opacity-[0] group-focus/hash:opacity-[0] md:group-hover/hash:opacity-[1] md:group-focus/hash:opacity-[1] -ml-6 pr-2"><a href="#group" aria-label="Direct link to heading" class="inline-flex h-full items-start leading-snug"><svg style="mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);-webkit-mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;-webkit-mask-position:center" class="gb-icon self-center transition-colors text-transparent group-hover/hash:text-tint-subtle contrast-more:group-hover/hash:text-tint-strong size-4"></svg></a></div><div class="flex-1 z-1 max-w-full break-words text-start justify-self-start leading-snug"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">group</code></div></h4><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">This sorts enchantments into specified groups from the <code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">groups.yml</code> file. It also hints at which global color should be used for display. Group names should be in UPPER-CASE.</p><h4 id="applies" class="text-xl font-semibold heading flex items-baseline scroll-mt-(--content-scroll-margin) text-start justify-self-start relative group/hash mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 column-first-of-type:pt-0 pt-[0.5em]"><div class="relative hash grid grid-area-1-1 h-[1em] border-0 opacity-0 group-hover/hash:opacity-[0] group-focus/hash:opacity-[0] md:group-hover/hash:opacity-[1] md:group-focus/hash:opacity-[1] -ml-6 pr-2"><a href="#applies" aria-label="Direct link to heading" class="inline-flex h-full items-start leading-snug"><svg style="mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);-webkit-mask-image:url(https://ka-p.fontawesome.com/releases/v6.6.0/svgs/regular/hashtag.svg?v=2&amp;token=a463935e93);mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;-webkit-mask-position:center" class="gb-icon self-center transition-colors text-transparent group-hover/hash:text-tint-subtle contrast-more:group-hover/hash:text-tint-strong size-4"></svg></a></div><div class="flex-1 z-1 max-w-full break-words text-start justify-self-start leading-snug"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">applies</code></div></h4><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">This sets a specified list of items that the enchantment can be added to. It supports plugin shortcuts such as:</p><ul class="min-w-0 space-y-2 mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0"><li class="leading-normal flex items-start"><div class="text-base leading-normal mr-1 flex min-h-lh min-w-6 items-center justify-center text-tint"><div class="before:font-var before:content-(--pseudoBefore--content)" style="--pseudoBefore--content:&#x27;•&#x27;;--font-family:Arial;font-size:min(1.5em, 24px);line-height:1"></div></div><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col space-y-2"><p class="page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl min-h-lh flip-heading-hash [h2]:pt-0 [h3]:pt-0 [h4]:pt-0 mx-0 text-start justify-self-start"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">ALL_[material]</code>: Sets all specified material items (e.g., <code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">ALL_SWORD</code> allows all types of swords).</p></div></li><li class="leading-normal flex items-start"><div class="text-base leading-normal mr-1 flex min-h-lh min-w-6 items-center justify-center text-tint"><div class="before:font-var before:content-(--pseudoBefore--content)" style="--pseudoBefore--content:&#x27;•&#x27;;--font-family:Arial;font-size:min(1.5em, 24px);line-height:1"></div></div><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col space-y-2"><p class="page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl min-h-lh flip-heading-hash [h2]:pt-0 [h3]:pt-0 [h4]:pt-0 mx-0 text-start justify-self-start"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">[material]_ARMOR</code>: Supports all types of specified armor (can be used with the previous shortcut to create <code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]">ALL_ARMOR</code>, allowing all possible armor pieces).</p></div></li></ul><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start"><code class="py-px px-1.5 min-w-6.5 justify-center items-center ring-1 ring-inset ring-tint bg-tint rounded-sm text-[.875em] leading-[calc(max(1.20em,1.25rem))]"><strong class="font-bold">effects</strong></code></p><p class="mx-auto page-width-wide:mx-0 w-full decoration-primary/6 print:break-inside-avoid max-w-3xl page-api-block:ml-0 text-start justify-self-start">Effects are the abilities which enchantment has. class="gb-icon ml-0.5 inline size-3 links-accent:text-tint-subtle"></svg></a></p><h3 id="other-options" class="text-2xl font-semibold heading flex items-baseline scroll-mt-(--content-scroll-margin)
